text # Known deviations from C# wade
chars 0+31:100,180,255/-/b
text 
text While the port is a strict behavioral 
chars 0+38:200,200,200/-/-
text clone (see docs/rust-port-plan.md), 
chars 0+11:200,200,200/-/- 11+22:206,145,120/-/- 33+3:200,200,200/-/-
text deliberate deviations are recorded 
chars 0+35:200,200,200/-/-
text here. The C# retirement trigger 
chars 0+32:200,200,200/-/-
text requires this file to be empty or fully 
chars 0+40:200,200,200/-/-
text accepted.
chars 0+9:200,200,200/-/-
text 
text ## Temporary (phase-scoped, expected to 
chars 0+40:86,156,214/-/b
text be removed by later phases)
chars 0+27:86,156,214/-/b
text 
text - Open external goes through cmd /C 
chars 0+2:180,180,180/-/- 2+34:200,200,200/-/b
text   start (Phase 4c). C# uses 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+17:200,200,200/-/b 19+9:200,200,200/-/-
text   UseShellExecute = true; Rust std has 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+22:206,145,120/-/- 24+15:200,200,200/-/-
text   no ShellExecute, so the Windows path 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+37:200,200,200/-/-
text   shells through cmd /C start and the 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+15:200,200,200/-/- 17+12:206,145,120/-/- 29+9:200,200,200/-/-
text   unix path is deferred to Phase 9 with 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+38:200,200,200/-/-
text   the rest of the unix work.
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+26:200,200,200/-/-
text 
text - The file-operation progress overlay 
chars 0+2:180,180,180/-/- 2+36:200,200,200/-/b
text   is enhanced (Phase 4c). C# shows only 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+23:200,200,200/-/b 25+15:200,200,200/-/-
text   the operation label; the Rust overlay 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+38:200,200,200/-/-
text   also shows the item count and current 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+38:200,200,200/-/-
text   file name. Deliberate divergence, so 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+37:200,200,200/-/-
text   no golden fixture covers this overlay.
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+38:200,200,200/-/-
text 
text - The filesystem watcher is a no-op on 
chars 0+2:180,180,180/-/- 2+37:200,200,200/-/b
text   unix (Phase 4d). C# uses 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+16:200,200,200/-/b 18+9:200,200,200/-/-
text   FileSystemWatcher on every OS; the 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+17:206,145,120/-/- 19+18:200,200,200/-/-
text   Rust watcher is hand-rolled on 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+31:200,200,200/-/-
text   ReadDirectoryChangesW and the inotify 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+21:206,145,120/-/- 23+17:200,200,200/-/-
text   backend is deferred to Phase 9 with 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+36:200,200,200/-/-
text   the rest of the unix work. Unix 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+32:200,200,200/-/-
text   listings refresh only on manual 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+32:200,200,200/-/-
text   refresh or navigation.
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+22:200,200,200/-/-
text 
text - Unix Read-only/ReadOnly follows std, 
chars 0+2:180,180,180/-/- 2+37:200,200,200/-/b
text   not .NET (Phase 4d). The Properties 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+20:200,200,200/-/b 22+16:200,200,200/-/-
text   overlay uses Permissions::readonly() 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+13:200,200,200/-/- 15+23:206,145,120/-/- 38+1:200,200,200/-/-
text   (no write bit for anyone); .NET 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+32:200,200,200/-/-
text   reports ReadOnly when the current 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+34:200,200,200/-/-
text   user lacks write permission. Revisit 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+37:200,200,200/-/-
text   with the Phase 9 unix work.
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+27:200,200,200/-/-
text 
text - Later-phase preview and metadata 
chars 0+2:180,180,180/-/- 2+33:200,200,200/-/b
text   providers are absent (Phase 7a). The 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+32:200,200,200/-/b 34+5:200,200,200/-/-
text   registries hold only the ported 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+32:200,200,200/-/-
text   providers, in C# order. Missing until

text ## Decisions (settled)
chars 0+22:86,156,214/-/b
text 
text - Motivation: native binary wins + 
chars 0+2:180,180,180/-/- 2+10:200,200,200/-/b 12+23:200,200,200/-/-
text   language consolidation. Not primarily 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+38:200,200,200/-/-
text   a learning exercise.
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+20:200,200,200/-/-
text - End state: temporary coexistence. C# 
chars 0+2:180,180,180/-/- 2+9:200,200,200/-/b 11+28:200,200,200/-/-
text   is deleted after Rust reaches parity 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+37:200,200,200/-/-
text   and has been daily-driven for 2 weeks.
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+38:200,200,200/-/-
text - Architecture: keep wade's raw VT/ANSI 
chars 0+2:180,180,180/-/- 2+12:200,200,200/-/b 14+26:200,200,200/-/-
text   design. No crossterm/ratatui. Port 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+35:200,200,200/-/-
text   the architecture, not just the 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+31:200,200,200/-/-
text   features: ScreenBuffer diffing, 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+32:200,200,200/-/-
text   IInputSource seam, no TUI frameworks.
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+12:206,145,120/-/- 14+25:200,200,200/-/-
text - Code style: fully idiomatic Rust 
chars 0+2:180,180,180/-/- 2+10:200,200,200/-/b 12+23:200,200,200/-/-
text   inside module boundaries. App.cs is 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+6:200,200,200/-/d 8+20:200,200,200/-/- 28+6:206,145,120/-/- 34+4:200,200,200/-/-
text   broken up; no 5K-line impl.
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+22:200,200,200/-/- 24+4:206,145,120/-/- 28+1:200,200,200/-/-
text - Behavior: strict behavioral clone 
chars 0+2:180,180,180/-/- 2+8:200,200,200/-/b 10+26:200,200,200/-/-
text   until cutover (keybindings, 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+28:200,200,200/-/-
text   rendering, config keys, quirks). 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+33:200,200,200/-/-
text   Deliberate deviations tracked in 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+33:200,200,200/-/-
text   src/wade-rs/KNOWN_DEVIATIONS.md. UX 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+31:206,145,120/-/- 33+5:200,200,200/-/-
text   changes happen after C# deletion.
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+33:200,200,200/-/-
text - Scope: staged to full parity. Core 
chars 0+2:180,180,180/-/- 2+5:200,200,200/-/b 7+30:200,200,200/-/-
text   browser first, exotic providers 
chars 0+2:-/-/- 2+32:200,200,200/-/-
text   later. End goal is 100% feature

text   parity, no silent permanent drops.

text ## fzf (junegunn)

text ### Core Algorithm
chars 0+18:78,201,176/-/b
text 
text Two fuzzy matching algorithms, 
chars 0+31:200,200,200/-/-
text selectable via --algo=v1 or --algo=v2 
chars 0+15:200,200,200/-/- 15+9:206,145,120/-/- 24+4:200,200,200/-/- 28+9:206,145,120/-/- 37+1:200,200,200/-/-
text (default):
chars 0+10:200,200,200/-/-
text 
text FuzzyMatchV1 — O(n) greedy:
chars 0+12:200,200,200/-/b 12+15:200,200,200/-/-
text 
text 1. Forward scan: walk input 
chars 0+3:180,180,180/-/- 3+25:200,200,200/-/-
text    left-to-right, matching pattern 
chars 0+3:-/-/- 3+32:200,200,200/-/-
text    characters in order
chars 0+3:-/-/- 3+19:200,200,200/-/-
text 2. Backward scan: from end position, 
chars 0+3:180,180,180/-/- 3+34:200,200,200/-/-
text    walk backward to find shortest 
chars 0+3:-/-/- 3+31:200,200,200/-/-
text    matching substring
chars 0+3:-/-/- 3+18:200,200,200/-/-
text 3. Score the found substring
chars 0+3:180,180,180/-/- 3+25:200,200,200/-/-
text 
text Fast but not optimal — finds the first 
chars 0+39:200,200,200/-/-
text occurrence, not the highest-scoring one.
chars 0+40:200,200,200/-/-
text 
text FuzzyMatchV2 (default) — Modified 
chars 0+12:200,200,200/-/b 12+22:200,200,200/-/-
text Smith-Waterman, O(nm):
chars 0+22:200,200,200/-/-
text 
text 1. ASCII fast-path index scan to 
chars 0+3:180,180,180/-/- 3+30:200,200,200/-/-
text    determine search bounds
chars 0+3:-/-/- 3+23:200,200,200/-/-
text 2. Compute bonus values for each 
chars 0+3:180,180,180/-/- 3+30:200,200,200/-/-
text    character position
chars 0+3:-/-/- 3+18:200,200,200/-/-
text 3. Fill score matrix Hm][n] — every 
chars 0+3:180,180,180/-/- 3+33:200,200,200/-/-
text    pattern character must match 
chars 0+3:-/-/- 3+29:200,200,200/-/-
text    somewhere
chars 0+3:-/-/- 3+9:200,200,200/-/-
text 4. Backtrace from maximum score position
chars 0+3:180,180,180/-/- 3+37:200,200,200/-/-
text 
text Falls back to V1 if N * M > slab 
chars 0+20:200,200,200/-/- 20+13:206,145,120/-/-
text capacity or M > 1000.
chars 0+8:206,145,120/-/- 8+4:200,200,200/-/- 12+8:206,145,120/-/- 20+1:200,200,200/-/-

text ### What's Wrong with Current Approach
chars 0+38:78,201,176/-/b
text

text    matching. "pdf" can never match

text    "report.pdf" because no segment

text    starts with "pdf" and edit distance 
chars 0+3:-/-/- 3+11:200,200,200/-/d 14+25:200,200,200/-/-
text    is too high.
chars 0+3:-/-/- 3+12:200,200,200/-/-
text 2. Damerau-Levenshtein is the wrong 
chars 0+3:180,180,180/-/- 3+33:200,200,200/-/b
text    tool. DL measures edits to transform 
chars 0+3:-/-/- 3+5:200,200,200/-/b 8+32:200,200,200/-/-
text    one string into another — designed 
chars 0+3:-/-/- 3+35:200,200,200/-/-
text    for typo correction on 
chars 0+3:-/-/- 3+23:200,200,200/-/-
text    similarly-sized strings, not for 
chars 0+3:-/-/- 3+33:200,200,200/-/-
text    finding a short query inside a long 
chars 0+3:-/-/- 3+36:200,200,200/-/-
text    path.
chars 0+3:-/-/- 3+5:200,200,200/-/-
text 3. Two-phase prefix+fuzzy is too rigid.

text    Real tools use a single scoring pass 
chars 0+3:-/-/- 3+37:200,200,200/-/-
text    where prefix/boundary matches

text    naturally score higher.
chars 0+3:-/-/- 3+23:200,200,200/-/-

text Agree)
chars 0+6:78,201,176/-/b

text    segment splitting.
chars 0+3:-/-/- 3+18:200,200,200/-/-

chars 0+46:86,156,214/-/b
text 
text All three tools match against the full path string and use subsequence matching (characters in 
chars 0+34:200,200,200/-/- 34+16:200,200,200/-/b 50+9:200,200,200/-/- 59+20:200,200,200/-/b 79+16:200,200,200/-/-
text order, gaps allowed) as the default. Scoring bonuses at boundary positions (after /, ., _, 
chars 0+82:200,200,200/-/- 82+1:206,145,120/-/- 83+2:200,200,200/-/- 85+1:206,145,120/-/- 86+2:200,200,200/-/- 88+1:206,145,120/-/- 89+2:200,200,200/-/-
text camelCase transitions) naturally make segment-start matches rank higher — without explicit 
chars 0+91:200,200,200/-/-
text segmentation.
chars 0+13:200,200,200/-/-

text ## How They Handle "pdf" Matching "report.pdf"
chars 0+46:86,156,214/-/b
text 
text The query characters p, d, f are found in order within report.pdf. The p gets a boundary bonus 
chars 0+21:200,200,200/-/- 21+1:206,145,120/-/- 22+2:200,200,200/-/- 24+1:206,145,120/-/- 25+2:200,200,200/-/- 27+1:206,145,120/-/- 28+27:200,200,200/-/- 55+10:206,145,120/-/- 65+6:200,200,200/-/- 71+1:206,145,120/-/- 72+8:200,200,200/-/- 80+14:200,200,200/-/b 94+1:200,200,200/-/-
text because it follows . (a non-word character). This makes it rank well without any special substring 
chars 0+19:200,200,200/-/- 19+1:206,145,120/-/- 20+79:200,200,200/-/-
text or extension logic.
chars 0+19:200,200,200/-/-

chars 0+3:-/-/- 3+8:200,200,200/-/- 11+11:200,200,200/-/d 22+37:200,200,200/-/-
text 2. Damerau-Levenshtein is the wrong tool. DL measures edits to transform one string into another — 
chars 0+3:180,180,180/-/- 3+38:200,200,200/-/b 41+58:200,200,200/-/-
text    designed for typo correction on similarly-sized strings, not for finding a short query inside a 
chars 0+3:-/-/- 3+96:200,200,200/-/-
text    long path.
chars 0+3:-/-/- 3+10:200,200,200/-/-

chars 0+3:-/-/- 3+31:200,200,200/-/-

text 
text 1. Subsequence match against the relative path — query chars must appear in order, gaps allowed. No 
chars 0+3:180,180,180/-/- 3+43:200,200,200/-/b 46+54:200,200,200/-/-
text    segment splitting.
chars 0+3:-/-/- 3+18:200,200,200/-/-

chars 0+3:180,180,180/-/- 3+27:200,200,200/-/b 30+25:200,200,200/-/- 55+1:206,145,120/-/- 56+2:200,200,200/-/- 58+1:206,145,120/-/- 59+2:200,200,200/-/- 61+1:206,145,120/-/- 62+36:200,200,200/-/-
text    penalty (-3 start, -1 extend).
chars 0+3:-/-/- 3+30:200,200,200/-/-
text 3. Filename priority (VS Code's best idea) — score query against just the filename; if it matches, 
chars 0+3:180,180,180/-/- 3+17:200,200,200/-/b 20+79:200,200,200/-/-
text    add large bonus.
chars 0+3:-/-/- 3+16:200,200,200/-/-
